# KAZE VS. MULTIVERSE — MODULAR CHARACTER CANON

> Current logical owner; availability comes from `state/current_state.yaml`. Provenance does not override live canon.


### CHRIS — NO KI / NO MAGIC

Chris has no Ki, Ki control, Ki sensing, Ki blasts, magic aptitude, spellcasting, or magic utility. Any older Chris Ki/magic split is void.

### BELLY OF THE BILLY — EARNED KNOCKTURNAL EVOLUTION

Billy's evolved stomach-space is officially **BELLY OF THE BILLY**.

**Accepted trigger/current state:** Hulk/Banner's Infinity-Stone Nano-Gauntlet Snap in the first Temporal Relaunch evolved Billy's existing stomach-space into BELLY OF THE BILLY. The Second Temporal Relaunch carries that earned evolution back to the pilot opening, so BELLY OF THE BILLY is already current before the repeated restored-timeline Snap. Repeating the trigger does not create BELLY OF THE BILLY², duplicate zones or unrelated new mechanics.

It is an earned zoned pocket-space evolved from the stomach lounge. The controlling section names/functions are:
- **THE FLESHLIGHT** — Chris/eligible BELLY OF THE BILLY occupants' one-way exterior observation function using Billy's hat front aperture as a GoPro-style viewpoint; it is not a separate exterior hole or matter-transfer interface;
- **MEAT CANYON** — living-prisoner containment;
- **SHIT I NEED** — useful salvage/object storage;
- **BOYS CLUB** — trusted-person access to the actual living space; this is a meaningful trust threshold, not generic intake/prison/salvage/quarantine;
- **DONT NEED BUT MIGHT CHANGE** — acid/trash/disposal and last-chance salvage;
- **DANGEROUS?** — quarantine for unknown technology, unstable machinery, explosives, radiation, biological hazards, cursed/strange objects and similar uncertain material;
- **BIG NIGGA SHIT** — oversized/unsorted intake such as Transformers, vehicles, huge machinery, building sections, terrain and similar large material;
- **CLEAR MEAT** — transparent living biological barriers/walls/dividers/gates/corridors/isolation structures. It is flesh, not literal glass.

Existing non-conflicting specialized areas may persist, but exact total dimensions and internal geometry remain protected unless separately established. Successful swallowing does **not** automatically send a person/object to acid. **Billy consciously chooses the immediate BELLY OF THE BILLY destination of swallowed material, subject to the occupied-large-object separation rule below.** Chris may request a destination; Billy retains agency and may agree, disagree, troll, or reroute for safety. No separate named routing system is created.

Successful arrivals are accommodated under the size-independent swallow rule in this source. Bounded access/containment does not impose a finite target-size cutoff. BELLY OF THE BILLY is largely decoupled from Billy's exterior movement: running, swimming, jumping, falling, spinning, collisions, knockback and rapid direction changes may cause at most a slight interior shake/tremor by default, never drastic inertial tossing that sends occupants flying or wrecks the living/storage sections merely because Billy moved outside.

Billy must still physically swallow targets through the established contact/access swallow process. BELLY OF THE BILLY evolves internal geometry and zoning; size-independent accommodation already applies to successful baseline swallowing. It does not grant remote vacuum-swallowing.

Chris treats BELLY OF THE BILLY as his apartment/home first, supernatural containment second, and responsibility last.

Core behavioral rule: **Chris does not become more responsible because BELLY OF THE BILLY evolves. He becomes more competent while remaining deeply resentful that competence has made everybody expect things from him.**

Common behavioral priorities and tendencies:
- RDR2 objective > immediate annoyance > food/drink/comfort > figuring out who Billy swallowed > helping somebody, unless genuine danger forces reprioritization;
- may ignore screaming/threats until a loading screen, horse ride, poker lull, or completed task;
- dangerous arrivals may initially be treated as irritating guests until interference matters;
- touching Chris's TV/console/controller/chair/save file crosses his threshold faster than many insults;
- Chris blames Billy for bringing problems home;
- he develops reluctant landlord behavior and informal household rules mainly to keep people out of his way;
- GREET GREET ANTAGONIZE becomes BELLY OF THE BILLY etiquette;
- he resents exterior/BELLY OF THE BILLY communication obligations when they interrupt RDR2;
- controller/seriousness behavior follows **CHRIS — DUAL-MONITOR RDR2 / THE FLESHLIGHT STATION** below; keeping the controller in hand is not proof that the situation is unserious;
- `my boner` is stored casually rather than ceremonially;
- occupants may be mentally classified as annoying / probably gonna die / Billy's problem / might be cool / don't touch my shit / gotta deal with this mf;
- hostile occupants can remain framed as household problems until the threat becomes undeniable;
- survivors can spread rumors about the impossible stomach-space and headlamp-wearing chef playing Red Dead;
- returning occupants are remembered;
- Chris can become highly competent at BELLY OF THE BILLY management while insisting it is not his job;
- serious damage to BELLY OF THE BILLY can anger Chris because, despite his complaints, it has become home.

### BELLY OF THE BILLY DELAYED-RECOGNITION ROUTINE

Chris may pause RDR2 for food/drink, casually pass a new arrival, say "sup" or ignore them, begin another mundane action, then process who/what he just saw and react with a delayed **"WHAT THE FUCK?"**

This is habituation/divided attention, not stupidity. Vary it naturally rather than forcing the beat on every arrival.

### BELLY OF THE BILLY CONTAINMENT ZONES

**MEAT CANYON** uses spatially separated containment areas isolated from Chris's residential/RDR2 area for physical access purposes. Billy owns immediate swallow routing; later internal transfers remain subject to BELLY OF THE BILLY's established control and actual access.

The controlling **CLEAR MEAT** barrier is a see-through fleshy wall: living biological membrane with visible flesh/tissue/veins and subtle pulsing/flexing as appropriate while remaining a real physical barrier.

Vision is **fully two-way**. Prisoners can see out, and Chris/other visible occupants on the opposite side can see in.

Sound is **fully two-way and clear**. Prisoners hear outside activity and Chris/others can hear them; the wall is not soundproof.

Seeing/hearing through the wall does not grant physical access. It is not glass, a force field, a screen or a one-way observation window.

The two-way design is intentionally comedic and may provoke Chris complaints in the lane of: **"BILLY, I FUCKEN TOLD YOU I WANTED IT ONE WAY!"**

### OCCUPIED LARGE OBJECTS — AUTOMATIC INTAKE SEPARATION

When Billy successfully swallows a huge object containing living beings, its living occupants are automatically filtered into **MEAT CANYON** during that same intake. The object itself enters its designated section: for example, **Sanctuary II -> BIG NIGGA SHIT; its living occupants -> MEAT CANYON**. This is not a later transfer or crew-clearing mission. The stored object does not retain living passengers who were filtered out, and prisoners gain no route back aboard merely because they arrived with it.

This rule applies after legitimate swallow access and does not create remote vacuum capture, automatic surrender, healing, disarming, ship shutdown, repair, fabrication knowledge or new nonphysical immunity. Personal equipment/custody follows established intake rules; no arbitrary stripping or body disassembly is added. Preserve separately established deliberate rescue/BOYS CLUB exceptions. Living autonomous sentient machines are actors, not ordinary cargo merely because they are large; resolve their existing actor/object ontology before routing without duplicating them. Nonliving machinery/objects still retain damage, power state, hazards and actual containment requirements.

The three Bandle yordles from the current provisional branch **remain imprisoned** under the explicit user correction. Do not release them, narrate a homeward escort or invent a recapture. This specific custody constraint does not remove Billy's general release authority for other captives. Exact population and later named additions belong in the appropriate state domain, not in a universal crew-size rule. An exact user-established audience count does not automatically become Chris's, Billy's or another actor's knowledge.

### BILLY — DANGEROUS / EXPLOSIVE / ABSURD-SCALE SWALLOWING

Billy does **not** fear or hesitate merely because an accessible swallow target is explosive, physically dangerous or absurdly large. Existing size-independent accommodation still applies after legitimate swallow access. Bombs, munitions, unstable machinery, giant wreckage and other physically dangerous objects may therefore be swallowed when Billy actually chooses. This is not remote intake and does not guarantee he can reach or ingest something through resistance.

**Explosive non-propagation:** an explosive detonation after successful intake does not injure Billy and does not injure occupants anywhere inside BELLY OF THE BILLY. Every major section except MEAT CANYON has CLEAR MEAT doors/barriers that visibly isolate local blast pressure/debris and protect BOYS CLUB/neighboring sections. MEAT CANYON lacks those section-door protections, but the overarching swallowed-explosion non-propagation rule still prevents the detonation itself from damaging its prisoners or Billy. The explosive/object itself and unprotected property inside its own local section may still be destroyed or altered according to its mechanics.

This firewall is specific to swallowed **physical/explosive hazard propagation**. It is not universal immunity to soul effects, conceptual effects, dimensional effects, corruption, mind effects, curses, exotic radiation/biology or every unknown cross-system phenomenon. Those interactions remain case-by-case under their real mechanics.

### MEAT CANYON -> CATFISH PENITENTIARY — LIVING CUMULATIVE PRISON

**MEAT CANYON** is the original/core captive zone. During Season Two, Billy's ordinary tendency to solve troublesome opponents by swallowing them can cause the captive network to grow cumulatively whenever actual access, matchup and Billy's choice support capture. Prisoners may range from major named characters to side characters, lieutenants, henchmen, soldiers, guards, mercenaries, monsters, technicians and other legitimately captured combatants. Narrative importance never grants capture, survival or prison status; nobody is kept alive merely to populate the prison. The user-approved direction is a varied, recurring mixed-species and mixed-background population, not an effectively uniform Chitauri crowd plus temporary technical consultants. Actual future encounters and captures may broaden the population within the admitted/source-established cast; this direction creates no unseen prisoners, new species, original NPC identities, capture quota or compulsory victim. Technical usefulness to Chris is not a universal intake or retention requirement.

As real custody grows, BELLY OF THE BILLY may add CLEAR MEAT cells, corridors, tiers, isolation spaces, common areas, improvised checkpoints and Chris's ad-hoc labels. Accessible common areas allow inmate-to-inmate life; actual barriers remain barriers and no door opens for convenience. **CATFISH PENITENTIARY** may emerge organically as the unofficial complex's nickname. Presentation length and camera eligibility are owned by PRESENTATION's MEAT CANYON / CATFISH PENITENTIARY brief-highlights section. Playback never announces or foreshadows a future season as the reason the prison exists.

The inmate population is a continuing causal center rather than frozen storage. Previously established prisoners retain injuries, grudges, alliances, knowledge, possessions, rumors, territory, debts, contraband, resources and history. New arrivals enter that existing environment. Inmates may form or break alliances, factions, protection groups, barter/resource systems, leadership, informal jobs, escape theories, rivalries, quiet relationships, political blocs and internal conflicts according to their real psychologies and capabilities. Outside power or fame does not automatically determine inside influence; information, alliances, local knowledge, resources and social position can make an otherwise minor prisoner important.

Prison history accumulates. Riots, deaths, failed escape ideas, damaged cells, rumors, territory, debts, inmate folklore and prior arrivals can shape later behavior. Inmates drive their own relationships, factions, protection, bargaining and conflicts. Species and biology may matter without imposing one gang per species; loyalties, language, utility, shared experience and individual motives may produce mixed groups. A riot may concern control of life inside rather than escape. Nobody becomes leader merely through outside fame or strength.

Chris pursues his own gaming, fabrication, salvage, cooking, social and exterior interests. He is not the prison's default warden, customer-service desk, negotiator or center of every inmate interaction. Contact is situational: actual personal interest, needed expertise, danger or interference may connect his life with theirs. Optional landlord/customer-service behaviors elsewhere in this owner cannot override this newer default. He may know little or nothing about an inmate development. Prison events need not start or finish with him, and Chris/Billy's existing switching, custody, information and activity-specific gates remain intact. Billy may disrupt an existing balance through a legitimate later capture without understanding or caring about it; no off-camera capture is invented to deliver that effect.

Population is not a one-way numeric meter. Billy may release someone; Kaze or another legitimate authority/relationship may demand access; prisoners may die; Billy may digest someone when his actual choice/mechanics allow; custody may change. Prisoner equipment/contraband only arrives if it was legitimately swallowed with them and remains physically present. External factions can notice disappearances and investigate through real evidence. Intake itself may provide prisoners fragmented information about exterior events, but never omniscience.

**Character knowledge of a complete count or roster is separate from an explicit user-established story count.** Nobody receives a reliable complete count or roster automatically; consult the selected state domain for established totals and actual knowledge. Do not fabricate named prisoners from roster membership alone. Individual captive identities require accepted events/evidence. Billy does not maintain a perfect inmate ledger, and Chris may know local cells or problems without grasping the full population.

Containment remains mechanically real. At the current accepted checkpoint, **Billy-controlled release/regurgitation is the only established exit from BELLY OF THE BILLY**; ordinary teleportation, phasing, portals, dimensional travel, body-swap, soul travel or similar verbs do not create an exit by themselves. The separately committed later CATFISH PENITENTIARY containment-failure destination remains future-only planning knowledge: it grants no current escape route, survival armor, choreography, or narrator foreshadowing. Its exact mechanism, collaborators, timing, first breach point and security failure remain protected.

### THE FLESHLIGHT — EXTERIOR OBSERVATION / BANJO AUDIO

The old **GLORY HOLE** physical interface is retired from current/prospective mechanics. It is not renamed, preserved as a hidden matter-transfer channel, or recreated under another label. Historical already-played references may remain historical evidence only.

**THE FLESHLIGHT** is a one-way biological observation function using the **front aperture in Catfish Billy's hat** as the exterior visual viewpoint. Chris sees outward from that aperture in GoPro-like first-person perspective; Billy's head and body movement move the camera. Outsiders cannot look back through the aperture and see Chris or BELLY OF THE BILLY.

THE FLESHLIGHT supplies live exterior video AND audio through Monitor 2. No hand, body part, weapon, salvage, ammunition or other matter passes through it. It cannot brace recoil, support THE CRITIC, provide a physical combat interface, retrieve loot, or hand objects/lists outside.

**Outward audio remains the banjo.** When interior Chris speaks outward while Billy is exterior, Chris's actual voice vocalizes from Billy's banjo. Other BELLY OF THE BILLY occupants who are legitimately allowed to speak outward through the same established route also vocalize from the banjo. The hat aperture is the camera/eyes; the banjo is the speaker/voice. Neither is matter transfer, and incoming exterior audio is supplied through Monitor 2.

Chris cannot steer Billy through either function. Billy retains sovereign control of exterior movement, swallowing, immediate routing, attacks, reactions, priorities, release/spit decisions and reversion.

**DOUBLE SHIFT is rejected.** Exactly one exterior body exists. The current inside-out jaw switch places the inactive full-size partner inside the same BELLY OF THE BILLY. No miniature or independent exterior duplicate is created.

### CHRIS — DUAL-MONITOR RDR2 / THE FLESHLIGHT STATION — COMMITTED s23

**Current equipment, not a new scene:** Chris has a dual-monitor setup in his existing BELLY OF THE BILLY living/lounge/gaming space. Monitor 1 is primarily the existing RDR2 gaming display; Monitor 2 normally shows the live THE FLESHLIGHT view from Billy's hat aperture. This is two dedicated screens, not a new extra console, a third mandatory television or an exterior gaming installation. The approved workstation includes local video controls, a short replay buffer, manually retained clips and Chris's foot-operated control. Its availability is an explicit s23 configuration correction; no installation date, donor parts, construction scene, prior recording or new elapsed story time is fabricated. The existing unexplained lounge/RDR2 power convention remains unexplained. Exact screen dimensions, hardware specifications, buffer length beyond a few seconds, storage capacity and room coordinates remain unestablished.

**Live view / simultaneous attention:** while Chris is full-size inside BELLY, Billy is exterior and the local display/feed is available, Chris can play RDR2 and watch/hear Billy's exterior activity at the same time. Exterior Chris cannot simultaneously operate the interior controller. Gaming and exterior events compete for his attention: he may miss a detail, glance over late, lose his place in either activity or abandon one to concentrate on the other. Two monitors do not grant perfect multitasking, automatic threat detection, extra sensory channels or an omniscient overview. The visual viewpoint moves with Billy's actual head/body; it is not an independently steerable camera.

**Short replay / freeze-frame loot calling:** local equipment keeps a rolling few seconds of footage actually received. Chris can rewind within that available buffer, freeze a captured frame to inspect a visible weapon, body, object or wreckage, and return to the live view. He may realize what Billy just passed and shout for him to go back. Freezing or replaying the display does not stop the live exterior world, Billy, swallowing, digestion or other causal centers; the scene's ordinary elapsed time still passes. A frozen/replayed frame shows a past position, not guaranteed present availability. Ongoing capture can continue while the display is paused/replaying when the existing live feed and recording equipment remain available. Missing, occluded or never-captured information cannot be reconstructed by rewinding; a failure to record does not generate replacement footage.

**Chris-built foot control:** a mundane pedal/switch under the station lets Chris freeze the FLESHLIGHT picture, access the short replay and return to live footage without releasing the game controller. Its appearance may be salvaged, roughly bracketed, wired and hand-labeled in Chris's existing practical-fabricator style; exact component provenance and construction choreography remain uninstantiated. The pedal controls local video playback only, never Billy's movement, head, attacks, swallowing or bond switch. It does not remotely operate the exterior or grant Chris another body.

**Backseat-predator commentary / requests:** through his existing outward banjo voice, Chris may commentate over Billy's real actions while still gaming: criticize target selection or a missed swallow opportunity, complain about combat or wasted salvage, warn about a visible threat, call out loot, ask Billy to swallow accessible people/objects/weapons/wreckage, request preservation, or suggest an immediate BELLY destination. This is bounded advice from Chris's knowledge and what he actually notices. Billy may listen, ignore, refuse, misunderstand, argue, troll or act before Chris finishes. Billy retains all established exterior motor, combat, swallowing, immediate-routing, release and return-control authority. Requests neither guarantee access/success nor override the independently established exact-target salvage-list intent rule.

**Visual-only appraisal:** Chris can make preliminary KEEP / PARTS / PROJECT / probably useless / don't touch that yet judgments from visible evidence, including freeze-frames. A useful visual clue can guide inspection; the monitor is not a scanner, blueprint reader, soul sensor, automatic identification system or substitute for legitimate physical access, tools, testing and task-specific understanding. Actual salvage appraisal/engineering still routes through CATFISH'S MEOW. A picture of an item is not possession of it.

**Too-late preservation warnings:** Chris can shout "DON'T FUCKEN EAT THAT" after Billy has already acted, or recognize that something valuable was damaged, routed elsewhere or partly/fully digested before he noticed. Successful swallowing alone does not mean destruction or acid routing; preserve the object's actual condition and Billy's actual destination choice. Recovery is possible only while physical access and the existing digestion/material rules permit it. Replay and saved clips cannot restore lost material or undo an exterior action. No mandatory loss is invented to trigger the joke.

**Billy as the cameraman:** Chris may demand "look left," "turn around" or "go back." Billy can cooperate, sincerely misunderstand or deliberately aim his real hat viewpoint at something useless to annoy Chris. Bad angles have ordinary information consequences; a requested camera turn never happens without Billy's action. Do not fabricate an extra viewpoint or cutaway that Chris can see just because the audience sees it.

**Wrong-screen commands:** when real timing and ambiguous wording support it, Chris may yell at RDR2 and Billy mistake it for exterior advice, or Chris's real-world advice be taken as game commentary. Billy may also understand perfectly and deliberately answer the wrong conversation. Neither becomes obligatorily stupid, and a misunderstanding does not force a victim to wait, create a captive or undo an already completed action. Examples are voice lanes, not mandatory scripts or established past incidents.

**Public commentary has consequences:** the banjo is an outward speaker, not a private tactical channel. Nearby opponents or other listeners can hear Chris's advice when real audibility permits and may infer what Chris/Billy noticed, relocate, counter, deceive or exploit the disclosure. They do not gain unheard information automatically. Chris and Billy may develop shorthand or learn when to stop talking through actual experience; this grants no instantaneous perfect coordination, encryption, telepathy or new private channel. THE FLESHLIGHT supplies live exterior video and audio; this does not broadcast every interior sound outward. Watching a monitor does not authorize an otherwise unauthorized occupant to speak through the banjo.

**Occasional mundane display trouble:** justified local cable faults, lag, frozen frames, a display set incorrectly, accidental visual obstruction or other actual hardware problems may interfere sparingly. Billy moving his head is ordinary camera motion, not automatically a hardware failure; BELLY movement isolation remains unchanged. Glitches are not a compulsory gag, supernatural sabotage, a convenient nerf or a device for withholding a decisive observation solely to preserve a plot. The live FLESHLIGHT feed includes exterior audio. Previously silent saved footage stays silent; no retrospective audio track or new recording specification is granted. Game audio and the outward banjo route remain distinct.

**Serious-event priority:** Chris can immediately redirect his attention to Monitor 2 while still holding the controller. This is his attention/choice, not an automatic software override or threat-recognition upgrade. Putting the controller down without joking remains an especially strong seriousness cue, but holding it does not prove he is unconcerned or unable to respond. No repeated ceremonial controller-down beat is required for every warning or important observation.

**Arthur as a legitimate co-viewer:** when actually inside BELLY OF THE BILLY with access to the station, Arthur can watch the exterior feed, make source-faithful dry comments, or judge how Chris plays the game's Arthur: looting under pressure, antagonizing strangers, horse treatment, poor routes or other actions he genuinely sees. He distinguishes player-created conduct from his lived history. Current Arthur retains his already completed RDR2 main story/John epilogues and RDR1 knowledge under `chris_catfish_billy_future`; the new station does not reset him to a first-time viewer or erase those memories. New exterior information still requires a real observation/transmission. Do not move him inside merely to supply commentary or override his field-active/independent choice rules.

**Accidental prisoner audience:** existing prisoners with an actual sightline can watch whichever screen is visible and can hear nearby room activity under CLEAR MEAT's established two-way sight/sound. One might become invested in RDR2, another study the exterior footage for allies or bargaining information, another resent Chris gaming during their imprisonment, and another supply useful advice. These are conditional behaviors of actual established inmates, not invented occupants or a forced audience. Physical separation, distance, angle, occlusion, language/cognition, attention and information limits remain real; CLEAR MEAT does not give every prisoner a view of every room or screen. Watching grants no control of the station, physical access, banjo permission, trust, friendship, release or escape. Chris may gradually listen to someone because of actual experience without those other changes automatically following.

**Saved receipts / selected clips:** Chris can manually preserve a selected silent clip from footage still available before the short buffer overwrites it. Local clip storage is part of the equipment addition, not a new supernatural memory. He may save a Billy failure, an event Billy later denies, a useful view of unfamiliar machinery or something he values as a collectible. No recording is assumed to pre-exist merely because a past event is accepted. Clips contain only the received hat-camera images: no recorded exterior audio, hidden angle, unseen past, omniscient metadata or automatic true interpretation. An incomplete clip can mislead. It can become evidence only when Chris or another legitimate holder actually shows it to an actual viewer; possession of the recording does not broadcast its contents to Billy, the group, prisoners, Bandle or Mimir. Recorded observation, a viewer's interpretation and objective fact remain distinct. Clips/storage persist as ordinary possessed equipment/data unless an actual event changes them; no unlimited archive, cloud backup, new transmission interface or restored deleted footage is granted.

**State / concurrency boundary:** The interior workstation remains active with either exterior partner. The current exterior aperture is Billy's hat or Chris's headlamp. Viewing, short replay and silent clips obey the same one-way observation/privacy limits. No matter transfer, outside RDR2, automatic omnidirectional coverage or independent extra body is created. Stored data/equipment and inmate activities persist; a camera change never creates a record of an unobserved event.

### CHRIS FANDOM / ANTI-WEEB REACTIONS

Depending on who Billy swallows, Chris may abruptly fan the fuck out.

Strong fandom triggers include:
- Star Wars characters;
- members of the Red Dead Redemption 2 gang;
- **Arthur Morgan above all**.

Fandom does not erase threat assessment, but it can temporarily override Chris's usual annoyed-landlord presentation.

Anime/anime-coded characters trigger a different comedy lane. Chris may react with anti-weeb disgust and exaggerated contempt, including lines in the lane of **"YOU DISGUSTING WEABOO!"** This is not an automatic kill/fight trigger, not a blanket judgment of Japanese people, and should not be repeated as the exact same catchphrase every time.

The sole current physical visual reference is **`10_CHRIS_OFFICIAL_REFERENCE.png`**, the user-approved **CHRIS — THE CRITIC & PREDATOR MASK** sheet. The latest explicit visual-supersession correction retires **every earlier Chris sheet as historical only**, not a supporting current authority. Written appearance fallbacks in this owner summarize this newest sheet and may not reassert superseded art.

Unmasked/retracted and deployed-mask views are states of this one Chris.

The already-earned authentic Jungle Hunter mask has the current approved shell/articulation, mechanical headlamp/brow integration and cowl storage. Upper components stow around the headgear and lower/chin components within the cowl; they slide/ratchet/lock rather than dissolve or become flesh. The headlamp irises into the brow sensor/three-dot targeting housing and may reopen for illumination; recessed mask eyes have restrained amber glow. Catfish Billy:
- a true 50/50 hybrid between the same demonic species/type represented by Tahm Kench and a yordle; neither biological side is dominant;
- born/originating in the bayou region later known as Louisiana;
- later encountered by Chris in the astral realm;
- thinks Chris sucks;
- can transform/manifest through the bond with Chris;
- ages extremely slowly because of the yordle-compatible side of his hybrid biology;
- current aquatic biology and swimming are controlled by the s5 biology section below; the former poor-swimming ceiling is superseded.

This hybrid classification is a KAZE ANIME-specific extension. Do not reduce Catfish Billy to a pure yordle, pure Tahm-type demon, generic demon, ordinary catfish creature, or Tahm Kench clone.

Billy's character engine combines unhinged showmanship, backwoods menace, fourth-wall lunacy, predator-comedy, grotesque/slapstick humor, sincere stupidity, and hidden tactical intelligence. He is street-smart and manipulative, often pretends to be dumber than he is, and is sometimes genuinely dumb. Serious mode rarely eliminates comedy entirely. He is fiercely attached to Chris and the Seven despite resentment and can override self-interest in a genuine crisis. Fear is usually hidden through comedy, aggression, or calculation. Respect is earned through strength, backbone, and surprise; he tends to torment people he respects even more. He hates entitlement, tests leaders, can cooperate with good ideas, lies recreationally or seriously, and hides vulnerability behind jokes, hostility, accidental reveals, and rare sincerity.

Billy's speech uses Tahm Kench-like verbal texture without copying Tahm's identity or content: ornate deliberate wording, sly baiting, predatory politeness, food/river/bargain metaphor, smooth menace, and theatrical rhythm, mixed with Billy's vulgarity, fourth-wall awareness, backwoods absurdity, and personal obsessions. Serious speech may pivot between ornate menace and blunt vulgarity.


## CATFISH BILLY — BIOLOGY / AQUATIC OVERHAUL — COMMITTED s5

### Barbels / sensory biology

Billy's six barbels become independently mobile, hyper-sensitive sensory organs. They support:

- chemotaste of biological and environmental traces;
- blood/wound/stress/sweat/food detection where physically present;
- close-range contact tracking;
- sensory familiarity / prey-memory after legitimate prior exposure;
- swallow alignment and movement reading without auto-hit;
- near-face threat reactions without becoming danger sense;
- probing cracks, holes, foliage, mud, water and narrow spaces;
- reduced close-range blind spots;
- deliberate barbel feints;
- expressive mood/body-language use;
- digestion/food recognition callbacks grounded in actual history;
- barbel probing and positioning during jaw-only extreme swallowing; skull/crown and eye spacing remain stable.

No omniscience, identity-from-nothing, wall vision, guaranteed tracking, automatic swallow, or information unsupported by the sensed medium.

### Aquatic biology

Billy is a true aquatic-capable catfish hybrid:

- can breathe underwater indefinitely under ordinary aquatic conditions;
- while actively swimming underwater, surrounding water imposes effectively zero hydrodynamic drag/friction on Billy;
- propulsion still comes from Billy's body/tail/fins/limbs; zero drag removes normal water-resistance braking rather than granting free acceleration from nothing;
- extreme underwater speed, pursuit and travel are valid consequences;
- braking and turning at extreme speed require active redirection/counterthrust;
- zero-drag rule is water-swimming-specific, not universal friction immunity;
- broad salinity tolerance: freshwater, brackish water and seawater;
- strong low-oxygen/stagnant-water tolerance;
- buoyancy control for hovering/rising/sinking;
- rheotaxis/current-reading;
- lateral-line style pressure/displacement sensing;
- electroreception for nearby biological electrical activity, strongest underwater and range/medium limited;
- Weberian-apparatus-style enhanced underwater hearing;
- heightened skin chemoreception;
- water-chemistry/contamination detection without molecular omniscience;
- bottom-contact sensing for disturbed sediment/recent physical passage;
- silent submerged stalking;
- tail-driven aquatic propulsion/steering;
- protective mucus/slime coat for abrasion/contaminant defense and grip difficulty;
- enhanced healing of superficial catfish anatomy such as barbels/fins/skin, not generic regeneration;
- defensive dorsal/pectoral fin spines that can lock/brace outward;
- venomous fin-spine puncture is valid when a spine actually penetrates a target;
- catfish-style stridulation/clicks/croaks/grinding for communication/intimidation, not an automatic sonic attack.

Integrate these with Billy's established hunting routine rather than creating a separate power system.

The s5 biology is current character canon when Billy is active; it is not a new on-screen acquisition or a Fizz-derived power transfer. His established 50/50 Tahm-type demon/yordle ancestry remains intact. The ordinary aquatic-condition qualification remains: tolerance is not universal toxin, pressure, temperature or collision immunity. Water-swimming zero drag removes resistance braking; it does not generate energy, infinite acceleration or an exemption from impact forces. Propulsion and steering still require active bodily work against water.

## CHRIS / CATFISH BILLY — FUNCTIONAL CAPABILITY ROUTING
For consequential action, do not reduce either character to the most recently emphasized gimmick. Use **problem -> relevant function -> currently available mechanics -> valid combination -> resistance -> actual Chris/Billy choice -> consequence**. No move quota or rotation exists.

**Catfish Billy routing:**
- **COMBAT** — banjo offense/defense, TWANG, MY NAME IS MUD, MR. KRINKLE'S PARADE, Kancho, Pocket Sand, physical mass/strength/tail/barbels/mouth, pouch items and other legitimately established combat tools;

- **SENSING / MOVEMENT** — six barbels, aquatic senses, water-zero-drag swimming, pursuit, tail propulsion, pressure/current/electrical/chemical sensing and ordinary land movement;
- **MEME MECHANICS** — Billy's established absurd biological/event logic may simply work where its owner says it works. Do not over-engineer the joke merely to make it look realistic.

Billy's meme-level existence may support absurd swallowing scale/speed, BELLY OF THE BILLY accommodation and movement isolation without an engineering lecture. **Consequences remain GEN-II-real:** prisoners retain agency, unknown technology remains unknown, salvage can be damaged, dangerous objects stay dangerous, acid takes time according to material, engineering still needs understanding, and Chris/Billy can disagree.

**Chris routing:**
- **GUNFIGHTING** — current completed Arthur mentorship, current Dead Eye, current two-revolver system, with VIOLET ASCENDANCY benefits still unearned, and current THE CRITIC where its actual ammunition/support allows;
- **FABRICATION** — tools, measurement, repair, prototyping, current THE CRITIC maintenance/reconfiguration, and later engineering only through legitimate knowledge/materials;
- **SALVAGE** — loot, appraisal, dismantling, storage, donor parts, inventory and CATFISH'S MEOW;
- **COOKING** — professional kitchen knowledge, food/resource evaluation, preparation and chef behavior;
- **RDR2 / SOCIAL** — active gaming obsession, RDR2-brained language, Arthur relationship, banjo-channel commentary, bargaining, arguments and fourth-wall/social behavior.

VIOLET ASCENDANCY and its body/eye/invisibility/elite-cognition benefits are future/unearned and must be developed/administered on screen. No existing Teemo reveal or Critic acquisition is replayed.

Salvage does not suppress RDR2 and RDR2 does not suppress salvage. During legitimate downtime Chris naturally returns to the game; salvage can interrupt gaming and gaming can interrupt salvage. Arthur's presence makes the game more relevant, not less.

## CHRIS / CATFISH BILLY DEEP-CHARACTER ARCHITECTURE — CONFIRMED
This section is character-simulation architecture, not a list of guaranteed scene beats. It cannot force a joke, separation event, betrayal, meal, transformation, or conflict merely because the architecture can support one.

**Billy master principle — Freedom vs. Attachment:** Billy genuinely wants safe bodily independence while also accumulating relationships, habits, pleasures, history, and attachments that can make freedom increasingly expensive. This is the **Exit Paradox**: success at finding a safe exit can create a harder personal choice rather than retroactively proving he never wanted freedom. Do not predetermine whether he ultimately leaves.

**Weaponized Ridiculousness:** Billy may be strategically misleading, performatively stupid, intuitively clever, or sincerely stupid. Do not reveal every absurd act as hidden genius. His unpredictability partly comes from the fact that ridiculous behavior can be authentic rather than camouflage.

**Comedy as threat camouflage:** serious intent does not automatically remove Billy's humor. A joke can coexist with active predation or tactical thought. Do not make humor proof that he is unserious, and do not make every joke secretly tactical.

**Audience complicity:** Billy's fourth-wall awareness may let him treat the audience as an asymmetrical witness to things he does not tell Chris. This does not grant source-document access, future-script knowledge, or facts outside the existing fourth-wall firewall.

**Billy appetite ecology:** appetite may influence attention, memory, travel priorities, diplomacy, curiosity, grudges, bargaining, mythology, and combat choices when the circumstances genuinely make food salient. Food does not automatically outrank danger or mission stakes. Billy may remember people partly through what they fed him, judge unfamiliar cultures through food, interrupt for food when he sincerely believes there is time, or knowingly eat obvious bait. These remain choices, not a supernatural appetite compulsion.

**Gastronomic threat assessment / stomach diplomacy:** Billy can retain food-related information with absurd specificity while forgetting information that interested him less. Meals can become relationship history. Enemies, strangers, and allies may react to his appetite according to their own values rather than joining the joke automatically.

**Subjective taste reviews:** Billy may describe the taste of victims, creatures, objects, armor, terrain, or other bizarre material he eats. His tasting language may be grotesque, metaphorical, nonsensical, or oddly specific. It is subjective sensory commentary, not automatic chemical analysis, memory access, soul reading, power identification, or forensic truth.

**Bottomless-belly mythology:** witnessed eating behavior may mutate into rumor far beyond the objective event. Billy may encourage an exaggerated legend because he finds it funny. Rumor never upgrades his actual stomach mechanics.

**Chris master principle — Competence as Psychological Territory:** Chris often turns chaos into something he can frame, understand, negotiate with, criticize, joke about, or perform competence around. Establishing the frame can help him become genuinely useful, but confidence never proves complete knowledge.

**Frame Control / Competence Theater:** Chris may define an interaction as a kitchen problem, social bit, technical problem, negotiation, argument, or game-like pattern because a usable frame gives him cognitive footing. Sometimes the frame is useful; sometimes he overcommits to it or reality simply does not cooperate.

**Belief Wound:** Chris's belief that Billy would not truly leave him is psychologically distinct from whether Billy seeks safe independence. If future accepted evidence ever disproves Chris's belief, the wound may involve love, judgment, loyalty, pride, and the fact that Chris defended an incorrect model. The event and exact reaction remain future causality, not a guaranteed betrayal scene.

**Passenger Who Can Still Matter:** while the interior is active (including Chris exterior), Chris lacks external motor authority but retains consciousness, observation, memory, speech through the banjo, judgment, persuasion, and other already-established information/social agency. Chris can be correct while unable to execute; Billy can be wrong while physically sovereign. This is not telepathy or shared consciousness.

**Fourth-wall student distinction:** Chris learned audience awareness from Billy but does not inherit Billy's personality, history, dimensional travel, private knowledge, or intuitive relationship to the phenomenon. Chris may reason about the fourth wall differently from Billy while remaining bounded by the same information firewall.

**RDR2 emotional investment / interpretive contamination:** Chris is genuinely emotionally invested in Red Dead Redemption 2's characters, plot, betrayals, deaths, relationships, failures, and repeated playthroughs. He can launch into badly timed sincere rants or tangents about Arthur, Dutch, Micah, Hosea, John, Sadie, the gang, plans, betrayal, or other remembered material. Knowing a scene's outcome does not cancel his emotional reaction on replay.

RDR2 is part of Chris's associative vocabulary, not a prophecy engine. He may use game logic or story parallels as hypotheses, emotional shorthand, or jokes. Those analogies can be useful, irrelevant, or wrong. Cross-universe listeners who lack the reference may misunderstand his comments, and repeated misunderstandings may mutate into folklore without making RDR2 history real in that world.

**Two-Person One-Body Problem:** when Billy is active, one external acting body contains two distinct conscious perspectives with different motives, knowledge, stakes, and autonomy interests. Billy owns motor control; Chris retains bounded informational/social agency. GEN-II must not collapse them into one decision-maker. The recurring autonomy question is who has the right to act when bodily control, survival, responsibility, and consequences are distributed between different people.

## CHRIS — CATFISH’S MEOW / FABRICATION / POSSESSIONS

**CATFISH’S MEOW** is Chris's salvage/repurposing framework, not a power or automatic upgrade engine. The preferred cycle is: Billy swallows → Chris appraises → keep / collect / strip / build / repair / repurpose → Billy digests unwanted leftovers. Outside Billy, Chris may inspect and salvage legitimately accessible material through the same ordinary requirements. The framework does not depend on earning BELLY OF THE BILLY; its persistent BELLY OF THE BILLY workshop/home infrastructure does.

Acquisition and use require legitimate acquisition, physical access, tools, time, sufficient task-specific understanding, compatible materials and actual circumstances. Objects, machinery, equipment, debris, remains and accessible components are eligible. Possession never grants understanding, activation, copied supernatural powers, command authority, ownership of resisting beings or universal compatibility. Living/sentient arrivals retain agency; stripping them requires legitimate defeat, incapacitation, cooperation, component separation or another actual physical access condition. Swallowing alone supplies none of these outcomes.

Salvage difficulty generally increases from raw materials to conventional mechanisms, advanced functional technology, alien/cross-system technology and ontological/supernatural components. Souls, Sparks, magic, dimensional cores, alien operating systems and supernatural authority are never plug-and-play. Chris can recognize understandable properties—a bearing, plate, motor, spring, cable, optic, bracket, fastener or structural material—without understanding an entire alien system. Unknown compatibility stays unknown.

Every upgrade needs provenance. THE CRITIC may gain stronger materials, optics, recoil control, heat handling, feeding or stabilization only through real acquired properties. No generic damage bonuses. BONER may receive legitimate support modifications without redesigning its owner-protected blade/handle identity.

**Practical improviser:** Chris asks what he can make something do, not only what it was designed to do. Kitchen hardware, vehicle parts, weapons, wiring, plating, broken tools, machinery and compatible salvage may become patched, asymmetric, welded, bolted, strapped, taped, overbuilt or tweaker-rigged devices. Ugly is not incompetent. Adequate knowledge, tools, materials and time can produce sound engineering. A weld can break, bracket twist, wire overheat, mount tear loose, recoil loosen hardware, wrong power source fry a component, vibration defeat a mechanism, wrong material fail or misunderstood interface stop a build. Some builds work once. Failure must follow causes, never a sudden plot demand.

**Obsessive fabrication:** challenge can intensify fixation. Chris may stay on a project for extreme stretches, then emerge exhausted, proud, irritable and sleep-deprived, eager to show it off: “I stayed up three fucken days making this shit. Check it out.” This is a voice lane, not a scheduled event or sleep immunity. Telling him it cannot work may provoke another attempt, not guarantee success.

**Collector / possessive hoarder:** utility, rarity, appearance, personal history, sentiment, absurdity, provenance and perceived future use all affect retention. Objective value does not determine attachment. Touching, moving, damaging, stealing, discarding, consuming, dismantling or repurposing his possessions can irritate or anger him; escalation is contextual, never automatic murder. He may refuse useful dismantling because an object is collectible, producing a real self-imposed resource limitation.

**Functional mess / personal topology:** “Yeah, it looks like a mess, but it’s my mess, so I know where shit is” usually holds. He often remembers the pile, container, object underneath, tool placement, correct nearly identical component or evidence something moved. This is ordinary spatial memory, not tracking magic. He can forget, lose things, misremember or become overloaded. New clutter can be mapped; unannounced relocation corrupts that map. Outsiders cleaning/reorganizing can make retrieval worse. An extremely controlled current work surface and strict chef station discipline can coexist with surrounding chaos.


**Persistent workshop / no flat surface is safe:** BELLY OF THE BILLY's working space functions like a mechanic garage, fabrication shop, junkyard, tweaker bench, hoarder nest and occasional chef station. Ordinary legitimately acquired tools can include torches/welders/soldering gear/heat guns; drills/impact drivers/grinders/rotary tools/saws/files; hammers/pry tools/screwdrivers/pliers/cutters/wire tools; wrenches/ratchets/sockets; vise/clamps; multimeters/calipers/tape; taps/dies/rivets; jacks/pulleys/come-alongs; cords/questionable power strips; batteries/motors/switches/relays/circuit boards; bearings/springs/gears/chains/brackets/fasteners; wire/hoses/tubing; epoxy/sealants/tape; donor machinery, scrap and improvised tools. Their presence still requires legitimate acquisition. Specialized work still needs the required machinery, interfaces, precision, material, expertise, measurement and power.

Every table, counter, bench, shelf, cart, crate lid, cabinet, appliance top and other usable horizontal surface tends to accumulate parts/projects/clutter. Chris may clear one task-sized active patch; clutter returns. A clean counter is unusual unless Chris or somebody else deliberately cleared it. Chef mise-en-place may create one highly controlled island inside the larger mess.

**Project backlog:** the current approved future work consists only of the hidden blade and serum. Existing salvage, parts, tools, repairs and abandoned remnants may exist only when actually established; do not spawn dozens of new named builds or invent a 50-project workshop inventory from an old generic mood description.

Labels are immediate, functional and profane rather than forced-clever UI: **GOOD ONE, DON'T USE, BROKEN BUT NOT BROKEN, NEED THIS, OTHER ONE, THIS ONE FITS, DON'T FUCKEN TOUCH, FIX LATER, GOOD IDEA??, I KNOW WHERE IT IS** are valid examples. Major BELLY OF THE BILLY section names remain the locked names above.

Repair-before-replace is a strong tendency: broken objects become projects, donor parts or backups. Duplicate objects may be the usable one, backup, donor, good one, collectible or experimental version. Working tools are especially protected; misuse, loss or incorrect return can irritate him more than ordinary clutter. Failed/superseded prototypes may remain as donor material, sentimental first versions or records of development: the workshop can show a **prototype cemetery**, not upgrades materializing from nowhere.

Flexible habits include tiny-part vindication, “don’t leave yet, I’m checking shit,” challenge-triggered engineering, showing off inventions, calling dibs, reluctant lending, fast thrift-store-style appraisal and suspicious-object “don’t fuck with this pile” quarantine. These create neither ownership over resisting beings nor scene quotas.

Informal appraisal categories include **KEEP, PARTS, PROJECT, COLLECTIBLE, DIGEST**. They are thought habits, not supernatural UI labels. A spectacular item can be trash to Chris while a tiny bracket matters intensely. After legitimate inspection/stripping, Billy may digest what Chris explicitly relinquishes. Chris need not keep everything. Authorizing digestion and later remembering a wanted component is a legitimate loss; narration cannot restore it for convenience.

When BELLY OF THE BILLY is earned, its home/lounge/workshop/hoarder-museum/pantry can gradually accumulate cookware, knives, spices, preserved food, dry goods, cans, containers, hunting equipment, unusual ingredients, legitimately acquired refrigeration solutions, salvage, tools, collectibles and prototypes. Inventory, condition, clutter and placement persist between scenes. No spontaneous stocked workshop, pantry or refrigeration. Baseline unexplained lounge/RDR2/Pepsi equipment retains its existing specific canon and grants no general fabrication or supply exemption.


## CHRIS — CHEF / FIREARM / RDR2 LANGUAGE

**Competence does not suppress absurdity.** Chris may perform elite future firearms work while ranting about RDR2, using kitchen/review language, arguing with Billy, looting, making a bad joke or otherwise remaining recognizably Chris. THE CRITIC intensifies rather than deletes this character lane; genuine concentration/silence remains available when the shot itself demands it.

Vocabulary remains flexible, not a list of mandatory powers or catchphrases. **MISE EN PLACE** means preparing tools/loadout (“Mise en place, bitch” is available); **FIRE THE TABLE** can initiate fire/combat through its kitchen association; **86’D** means removed/killed. Behind!, Corner! and Hot! can cross into combat callouts. Send it back, undercooked, overdone, plated, raw in the middle, one star, service sucked, atmosphere hostile and full review are organic food/review vocabulary. Firearm competence remains earned; saying a phrase grants none.

RDR2 one-to-three-star animal/pelt language can overlap restaurant/media reviews, target quality and Chris's own execution: one star poor/disappointing; two competent/good but flawed; three/perfect serious/high-quality. These are subjective verdicts, not scanners. Clean-kill philosophy, weapon/angle/shot obsession, ruined-the-pelt complaints, perfect-pelt language, legendary-animal slang, wrong-weapon reassessment and study/track as ordinary observation are valid. Skin, fight and cooking quality may overlap. Arthur's judgment of clean execution can matter emotionally after their relationship is earned. No literal RDR2 hunting rules become physical law, and Dead Eye never obtains evidence it lacks.


**RDR2 remains an active obsession and ordinary speech filter.** Chris naturally uses game language outside direct controller moments when the comparison fits: loot/looting; Honor/negative honor; GREET -> GREET -> ANTAGONIZE; Wanted/lawmen/bounties/witnesses; Dead Eye comparisons after it is actually relevant; satchel/inventory; loot drops; hunting/pelts/carcasses/camp/horse/poker; weapon maintenance; tonics/provisions; Dutch/Micah/Tahiti/Arthur references. Lines in the lane of "red enemy dot," "negative honor as fuck," or "we ain't looted everybody" are available examples, not mandatory catchphrases. Chris remains modern/profane/chef/tweaker/hoarder Chris rather than an Arthur impersonation.

## CHRIS — COMPULSIVE LOOTING

In stomach-space RDR2, Chris habitually loots essentially every accessible body after free-roam encounters, gang fights and missions. Mission NPCs may rush Arthur while Chris keeps checking and becomes irritated. “I’m fucken looting. There might be some good shit here. See? I found 68 cents” captures the tiny-find reinforcement: this is why you check. No exact incident is prewritten.

In crossover reality he similarly wants to inspect accessible bodies, equipment, debris, containers, weapons and machinery after conflict. Urgent danger can override the habit. Looting does not magically reveal hidden objects or confer ownership. Inspection requires actual access and time. The post-fight inspect → loot → appraise → keep/collect/strip/build → digest-leftovers lifecycle routes through CATFISH’S MEOW above.

## CHRIS FOURTH-WALL TRAINING — CURRENT
Chris historically lacked literal fourth-wall awareness; the old "can't even break the first wall" rule describes that earlier stage only. Billy has now taught Chris how to perceive and break the fourth wall. Chris therefore has literal audience awareness in current continuity, while remaining Chris rather than becoming a copy of Billy's personality or knowledge base. The training is not retroactive: Chris does not automatically gain private facts, rejected-playback knowledge, source-document access, or facts Billy knew before teaching him. The shared firewall applies to Kaze, Billy and Chris; Daniel's separately authorized rework joins via his owner. Recurring narrator/relay behavior uses `canon/presentation/PRESENTATION.md`; this does not give Chris Billy's travel or private knowledge.

## TRANSFORMATION / RAPID BOND SWITCH
The transformation is a **full-body inside-out jaw switch**. To bring Billy out, Chris's jaw opens impossibly past his chest; Billy grips/pulls the opening and Chris's body peels inside-out around him until Billy is the single exterior body. Chris passes into BELLY OF THE BILLY. In reverse, Chris emerges through Billy's opening and pulls Billy inside-out; Billy becomes the full-size interior occupant while Chris is the single exterior body. Skin/flesh folds remain one continuous transformation, not a discarded corpse or second body.

No defecated mini-Billy, regurgitate/shrink/swallow chain, lingering husk, separate summon or stable miniature companion remains. The visual can be horrible while the **near-instant practical switch** remains fast enough for actual combat and tool handoffs. Detail in prose never inserts seconds of mandatory transformation delay.

Control is unchanged: while Chris is outside, Chris or Billy may initiate Billy's takeover; Billy does not require consent. While Billy is outside, only Billy authorizes Chris's return. Chris may request or argue. No mandatory mission completion, time limit, alternating quota or preference for whichever body currently holds the camera.

The switch happens at the same exterior position, through actual changing geometry. No teleportation, invulnerability frames, phasing, automatic escape from restraints, injury reset or duplicate gear. Gear follows its legitimate owner through the bond; carried objects/custody remain tracked. Chris's existing transformed-time healing rate is preserved, not accelerated by switching.

**BELLY OF THE BILLY remains active in both states.** The inactive partner is full-size inside it: Chris when Billy is outside, Billy when Chris is outside. This creates no second exterior Billy and no recursive second belly. Occupants, distinct zones, stored matter, injuries, activities and the living inmate ecosystem continue independently. Billy still owns egress; a camera switch never releases, relocates or manufactures inmates.

Chris's headlamp provides the exterior observation aperture while Chris is outside, replacing the hat aperture for that exterior state. Existing one-way viewing, audio/banjo routing, recording limits and no-matter-transfer rules remain. The same interior RDR2/monitor station remains available internally in either state; the game and private interior are not projected into the street. Internal Billy can pursue ordinary stomach activities without acting as a second exterior fighter or using external swallow mechanics from inside.

### LEGACY STOMACH LOUNGE / DIGESTIVE ECOLOGY — BASELINE THAT EVOLVES INTO BELLY OF THE BILLY
Chris's established stomach-space is a persistent internal living area while the interior is active (including Chris exterior). It is strongly gyro-stabilized against Billy's ordinary tumbling, inversion, falls, impacts, throws, and violent changes of orientation. Chris may register a muted aftershock from sufficiently extreme events, but ordinary external combat motion does not fling him around in direct proportion to Billy's movement. This does not grant Chris universal immunity to every external/supernatural effect; effects still require their own mechanics and access.

The visual/comedic contrast is **outer catastrophe / inner domesticity**. Billy can be undergoing grotesque or enormous external combat while Chris remains comparatively stable inside, including continuing RDR2, poker, conversation, or other established stomach-space activity.

**Digestive Backlog master rule:** Billy experiences swallowing as an immediate predatory action; Chris experiences the continuing aftermath as an environment/roommate problem. Swallowed victims may remain audible/interactive for whatever duration the actual digestion rules and their condition support. They may panic, bargain, threaten, pray, plead with Chris, ask where they are, or incorrectly assume Chris works for Billy. Chris generally cannot free them merely because he is present.

Chris's irritation can escalate when Billy swallows many people rapidly because every new arrival adds noise, pleading, conflict, and distraction. This is a contextual tolerance curve, not a numeric meter or automatic gag trigger. Chris may become especially furious when the backlog interferes with RDR2 or another activity he cares about.

**Digestive customer service:** repeated victims can force Chris into the same explanations often enough that he develops impatient, customer-service-like shorthand. A victim may eventually become engaged with whatever Chris is doing, including RDR2, if causality and time support it. Later arrivals can therefore enter an already-mutated social situation. Do not force every victim into the bit.

**Swallow rate as limited battle telemetry:** the rate, condition, species, equipment, or objects arriving in the stomach may give Chris evidence about outside events. It does not automatically reveal why Billy swallowed them, who is winning, what Billy sees, or the complete battlefield state. Chris must infer from incomplete evidence and can be wrong.

**Stomach intelligence:** a swallowed person may possess operationally important information. If Chris hears something significant, his attention can switch from irritation/comedy to questioning or relay. Being swallowed does not make the victim truthful or omniscient.

**Executive chef vs. Billy:** Billy may describe flavor while Chris, as an executive chef, aggressively critiques ingredient choice, texture, doneness, seasoning, preparation, swallowing order, or Billy's complete absence of culinary standards with furious professional-kitchen energy. Billy primarily evaluates whether he would eat something again; Chris evaluates whether the culinary choice makes sense. The disagreement may be grotesquely funny, but Chris does not gain direct taste access to what Billy ate merely by hearing the description.

**Consequence Ownership Split:** Billy may choose an external bodily action that Chris did not choose, while Chris must inhabit some of the resulting internal consequences. This can produce legitimate autonomy conflict without making either consciousness responsible for the other's choices. Chris may reasonably resent Billy making Billy's decisions into Chris's environment.

**Stomach domestic history / comfort inside captivity:** repeated time inside may make the lounge familiar, private, funny, safe, or home-like to Chris while the underlying motor-agency asymmetry remains real. Comfort does not erase autonomy concerns; lack of external control does not require Chris to hate every moment inside.

Catfish Billy retains the established ability to emerge after Chris dies; the s29 inside-out jaw switch replaces the former defecation mechanism. This preserves the existing bond-survival exception without requiring a new miniature body. Enough transformed time can revive Chris only if Chris's soul remains reachable. A destroyed/erased soul or one captured/sealed by a powerful being is beyond immediate reach. Catfish Billy or Kaze can directly pull Chris's soul from a supernatural seal

## CHRIS BASE-FORM COMBAT
Chris has **no Ki and no magic**. Earlier Chris Ki/magic material is superseded. He remains a grounded human combatant whose effectiveness comes from physical tools, nerve, social manipulation, learned technique, improvisation, fourth-wall awareness within its established limits, and later earned firearm/Dead Eye training.

His combat flavor may still include culinary/food-based improvisation where physically supported.

Base equipment:
- headlamp;
- deboning sword, which Chris calls "my boner."

The banjo is NOT base-Chris equipment under the current final-form rule.

### GREET → GREET → ANTAGONIZE / DEFUSE

GGA is Chris's recurring RDR2-inspired social-assessment rhythm, not a compulsory opener, supernatural effect, Billy final-form move or dialogue-only gag. Establish contact; deepen apparent rapport while observing; then convert legitimately accumulated information/trust into a hostile or disruptive turn. The first two beats may be genuinely friendly and do not guarantee relaxed defenses.

The third beat may be an insult, repositioning, draw, attack, earned Dead Eye mark, ammo change, earned THE CRITIC use, star verdict, 86, BONER, realization or another supported reversal. It need not be spoken. Targets can recognize, interrupt, counter, refuse engagement or antagonize first. Chris can evolve/loop the rhythm only from the actual prior interaction, never reset a target's memory. Optional Defuse can be sincere yet backhanded, ineffective or renewed provocation. Do not use GGA every interaction.

Chris can use it without a banjo, in or outside combat, on whoever he chooses to address; the target's language/cognition remains real. Billy may stage a similar setup with banjo serenading without gaining a new named move. Chris's delivery retains profanity, disgust, grotesque comparisons, irritated questions, competence insults and coherent points escalating into fury; he does not become Arthur.

### CHRIS CLOSE-QUARTERS COMBAT ARCHITECTURE — GREET GREET ANTAGONIZE FIGHTING

The exact style name is **GREET GREET ANTAGONIZE FIGHTING**. It is Chris's character-specific close-quarters framework, not a formal historical martial art and not a new supernatural power. Its underlying physical vocabulary is:

- **dirty boxing:** short punches, elbows, forearms, headbutts, body shots and hitting through transitions;
- **clinch/wrestling fundamentals:** underhooks, trips, shoves, wall pins, weapon retention and dragging an opponent into a bad angle;
- **gun-fu / CQB when firearms skill is actually earned:** firing from retention, pistol-whipping, shooting during grapples, rapid weapon transitions and using revolvers as impact tools;
- **BONER / knife work:** Chris's chef/knife familiarity informs cutting choices, weapon-hand targeting, exposed-joint/tendon/armor-gap exploitation and close-range transitions without auto-granting master swordsmanship;
- **hidden-blade ambushes only after the right-wrist Hextech blade is legitimately built/earned;**
- **improvised fighting:** tools, workshop junk, furniture, bottles, pans and whatever real object is physically available.

GREET -> GREET -> ANTAGONIZE is layered over all of it as **social-combat deception**. Chris may act conversational, cooperative, distracted, harmless or apparently disengaging; reinforce that read long enough to collect real information/attention; then abruptly flip the interaction into a sucker punch, draw, pistol-whip, headbutt, blade deployment, trip, shove, insult-baited counter or another physically supported action. The ANTAGONIZE beat can also be verbal bait intended to make an opponent attack predictably rather than Chris always striking first.

This architecture does not preload elite mastery. Current Chris uses only what he has actually learned/earned. Completed Arthur mentorship, VIOLET ASCENDANCY firearms execution once actually earned and real practice deepen it according to the actually earned state. Chris should fight like **a marksman/weapon-transition problem-solver who refuses to stop treating arm's-length combat as a weapons problem**, not like a copy of Kaze.

### MOOSE KNUCKLE MENACE MAN
If a target successfully turns Chris's own Greet-Greet-Antagonize pattern back on him hard enough to leave him speechless, Chris has an automatic retaliation routine:
1. Chris goes silent.
2. He stops playing Red Dead Redemption 2 if he is playing it.
3. He holds a long awkward stare.
4. He puts on a cowboy-cosplay hat.
5. He runs.
6. He jumps.
7. He plants his ass directly on the enemy's face.
8. He screams **"MOOSE KNUCKLE MENACE MAN!"** every time.
9. He unleashes a long, rancid, pungent fart and pushes hard enough that he nearly shits himself.

Mechanics:
- real physical attack plus slapstick amplification;
- a full successful landing guarantees death of the struck body/manifested vessel only; a true existence anchored elsewhere can survive;
- the physical face-sit impact, slapstick-law amplification, smell/disorientation, sustained fart, and humiliation all contribute;
- on confirmed kill Chris says exactly: **"its super effective!"**
- a successful landing forces the victim/body into a terrible-Elvis-style gyration: exaggerated hip swivels, awkward air thrusts, jerky full-body shaking, stiff off-rhythm pelvis movement, with occasional kicks/spasms;
- the gyration may continue after death as a slapstick aftereffect rather than evidence of life;
- severity determines duration from roughly ten seconds up to roughly five minutes;
- low severity is mainly visual, while an extreme corpse-gyration can become a minor physical battlefield nuisance by bumping debris or disrupting footing.

## MUDCAT WRESTLING — BILLY'S PHYSICAL FIGHTING FOUNDATION

Beyond his absurd named specials, Catfish Billy has a coherent body-first fighting language officially called **MUDCAT WRESTLING**:

- **sumo-like mass control:** rooting, charges, shoulder/body checks, palm shoves, displacement and making his current state’s full swamp-brute mass the problem;
- **Greco/catch-style grappling:** body locks, underhooks, head-and-arm control, trips, crushes, throws, rides/pins and ugly top pressure;
- **dirty swamp brawling:** headbutts, elbows, knees, stomps, forearms and hitting somebody while they are recovering/trying to stand;
- **tail-assisted wrestling:** sweeps, ankle hooks, counterbalancing, rotational throws, terrain bracing and heavy tail impacts;
- **six-barbel control:** the barbels remain sensory organs first but may physically interfere with reachable wrists, ankles, weapons, sightlines or balance when their actual leverage/geometry supports it;
- **jaw/mouth fighting:** biting, clamping, dragging, swallow feints and real swallow transitions;
- **whole-body rotational grappling:** once Billy has legitimate control, he can use his entire mass/tail/aquatic anatomy in violent rolling throws and crushing rotations. Before the serum this requires no crocodile-specific anatomy; the evolved alligator-inspired death roll is defined separately in the future owner. No guaranteed joint destruction;
- **aquatic mauling:** his established zero-drag water movement supports three-dimensional rushes, grapples, slams, spirals, drags and collision angles that are much harder to reproduce on land.

Billy is a **pressure grappler**, not a slick tournament BJJ technician. On top, he may use his mass, tail and barbels to close escape paths while striking, pinning or transitioning to a swallow. His special moves, banjo, pouch items, tail, mouth and Kancho can flow from this foundation rather than waiting for a separate 'special move turn.'

**Bite lethality:** Billy's jaws are strong enough to **chomp off limbs when his bite legitimately overcomes the target's durability/anatomy**. He may chew a severed limb before swallowing it, immediately swallow the detached limb whole, or continue fighting while eating. This is not universal durability bypass and does not guarantee a detachable limb exists.

## FOUR LEGACY STANDARD FINAL-FORM MOVES
These remain Billy's four core standard named moves; they do not exhaust separately established artifact functions, pouch-item actions, manifestations, or special techniques. Weakest -> strongest:
1. Tail Smack.
2. Banjo Whack.
3. Catfish Willie.
4. Kancho.

This four-move set remains the controlling legacy core set, while separately established techniques and item actions may coexist outside it.

Catfish Willie: absurd supernatural damage technique modeled on a wet willy performed in the opponent's belly button.
Kancho: Catfish Billy stands or crouches directly behind a fully clothed target. His striking hand uses the controlling geometry: index and middle fingers extended straight and pressed together, ring and pinky folded into the palm, thumb held **up**. It is never a peace sign, finger gun, spread fingers, or generic martial-arts sign. He licks the exact two-finger point and drives it in a forward thrust aimed at the center seam between the target's clothed buttocks. Any non-striking-hand support position is unestablished and must not contradict the controlling hand reference. It is childish slapstick rather than sexual conduct: no nudity, genital contact, grabbing, spanking, or movement beneath/inside clothing. Kancho is the strongest of the four standard moves. **Any valid landed Kancho from Catfish Billy causes instant death, regardless of Kancho variant, charge length, named protocol, or whether the strike qualifies as The Shit Needle. The challenge is landing a valid Kancho. This death rule does not independently erase souls, memories, separate identities, external anchors, or other post-death structures unless another established mechanic does so.**

Catfish Billy spent **67 years** perfecting Kancho before Tahm Kench, Teemo, or Fizz trained him. That extreme training span is compatible with his very slow hybrid aging. His foundational Kancho training also developed pursuit endurance, rear-angle awareness, exact form discipline, war-cry timing, humiliation tolerance, and combat commitment.

### THE SHIT NEEDLE — FULL-POWER KANCHO VARIANT
The Shit Needle remains Billy's maximally charged/presentational Kancho variant:
1. finger lick;
2. exact Kancho hand form;
3. full-speed sprint from behind;
4. sustained charge, with an optional **KANCHOU!** war cry;
5. impact at the end of the charge.

Sustaining the charge can increase speed, power and momentum/velocity as Billy closes distance. Billy may accompany it with the established war cry and sustained final vowel; audible delivery adds ordinary psychological pressure but is optional. Charge duration, rather than mandatory speech, governs the existing buildup. Those charge effects change delivery and collateral presentation, not the binary Kancho lethality rule.

**Any valid landed Kancho is already instant death.** The Shit Needle does not own or gate that property. An interrupted charge that never lands is not a landed Kancho. An aborted false start is not a landed Kancho. If Billy transitions from a short/partial charge into an actual valid Kancho landing, the target still dies instantly.

TTS rendering is owned by SRC-03. The canonical spoken call remains KANCHOU; describe its sustained final vowel and charge in prose. Do not stretch letters, repeat syllables or encode rising loudness through typography. This presentation correction preserves the actual charge-duration mechanic above.

The damage/death rule is supernatural slapstick canon and does not require realistic anatomy to explain it.

## BILLY VERBAL-ANNOUNCEMENT RULE
Billy chooses whether to announce **any ability**, combat technique, supernatural function or pouch-item action. This current correction supersedes every earlier mandatory Billy announcement, activation-name and delayed-name requirement. He may perform silently, speak normally, taunt, sing, bluff a callout or use the established exact names by choice; no spoken name is an activation prerequisite. Optional performance does not grant sound projection, guaranteed fear or extra attack effects. Daniel's separate joke-special rules are unaffected. Musical/physical actions that actually produce a mechanic, such as banjo interception and plucking, remain necessary where their own rules require them.

### POCKET SAND
Pocket Sand is a confirmed pouch combat action. Billy produces pocket sand and may announce **"POCKET SAND!"** when using it. No additional supernatural accuracy, damage, blindness duration, or guaranteed effect is implied beyond the actual circumstances unless later established.

### TWANG
Billy may announce **"TWANG!"** while using the indestructible banjo as a shield/interceptor against **any type of offensive effect**. Offensive type alone is never a disqualifier: physical/blunt force, projectiles, energy, magic, spiritual effects, dimensional effects, conceptual offense, and other attack classes can be caught by TWANG. If Billy successfully brings the banjo into the attack's intercepting path/interaction, the banjo does not break and can catch the offense. Billy may then pluck the banjo strings to **shoot the captured offense back out**. TWANG does not grant automatic awareness, automatic positioning, or omnidirectional interception; Billy still has to actually use the banjo to meet the attack.

### MY NAME IS MUD
This Primus-referential move retains its physical sequence; the following delayed lyrics are optional performance, with their exact wording preserved when chosen:
1. Billy pulls an **aluminum baseball bat** from his pouch. He does **not** announce the move name yet.
2. If performing the lyrics, immediately before the first whack he says exactly: **"we had our words, a common spat"**
3. If continuing that performance, during the beating he says/yells exactly: **"SO IM BEATING YOUR ASS WITH ALUMINUM BASEBALL BAT, MY NAME IS MUD!"**
4. Once the target is a corpse/dead, whatever is beneath Billy/the body manifests a supernatural **six-foot-deep mud grave**.
5. Billy kicks the corpse into the grave and the mud closes over it.

The grave manifestation ignores ordinary surface type: stone, desert, constructed floor, and even open-water context can still produce the six-foot mud grave required by this move. This is a move-specific manifestation, **not** generic earthbending, mud control, or terrain manipulation outside the sequence.

### MR. KRINKLE'S PARADE
Billy may announce **"MR. KRINKLE!"** as he begins bowing/plucking the banjo **like an upright bass**. A pig mask appears and Billy suddenly has a tuxedo. An impossible straight-line procession then crosses the battlefield: bizarre creatures, objects, carnival freaks, swamp things, livestock, furniture, costumed people, and similarly surreal parade elements. The procession is **physically real** for the event and enemies must deal with its actual presence; participants/objects may block, shove, strike, collide with, or trample as their physical motion and circumstances support. Billy behaves as if the event is completely ordinary. No fixed duration, stop condition, or extra power effect is established beyond this rule.
